### [What is Neu Deals?](https://neudeals.co/\#)

Neu Deals is your go-to destination for discovering the latest and most reliable coupons and deals across a wide variety of categories ranging from fashion and electronics to travel and daily essentials. Our mission is to help you shop smarter by providing easy access to verified offers, exclusive discounts, and unbeatable savings, all in one place.

### [Are the Deals and Promo Codes on Neu deals Updated Regularly?](https://neudeals.co/\#)

Yes, we strive to provide the most up-to-date deals and promo codes. Our team frequently updates offers to ensure you have access to the latest savings opportunities.

### [How Does Neu Deals Work?](https://neudeals.co/\#)

At Neu Deals, saving money is simple and seamless. Just browse through our curated collection of the latest coupons and exclusive offers across a wide range of categories. When you find a deal that suits your needs, click to either reveal the coupon code or activate the offer. Then, follow the provided instructions to apply the discount directly on the retailer’s website and enjoy your savings instantly.

### [How do I Use a Promo Code from Neu Deals?](https://neudeals.co/\#)

Simply click on the deal or promo code you want to use, copy the code provided, and apply it during checkout on the retailer’s website. Make sure to follow any terms or conditions mentioned for the offer.

### [How can I contact customer support?](https://neudeals.co/\#)

If you have any questions, concerns, or need assistance, our customer support team is here to help. Please visit the Contact Us page on the Neu Deals website and complete the form provided, We’ll get back to you as soon as possible.

## Why Shop with Coupons and Discount Codes?

Using coupons and discount codes is the smartest way to save money on your favorite products and services. By applying these codes at checkout, you can enjoy significant discounts, special offers, and sometimes even free shipping. It’s a simple and effective way to stretch your budget and get more for your money.

### Featured Categories:

- **Fashion and Apparel**: Get access to new hidden deals for all sort of clothing, shoes and accessories from leading stores.
- **Electronics and Gadgets:** Get the most out of your money on the newest electronics such as smartphones, laptops, and home theatres.
- **Beauty and Personal Care:** Discover skincare, make up, hair products and so much more on sale.
- **Home and Garden:** Secure affordable prices with free delivery on furniture, decor, kitchenware, and the gardening tools.
- **Health and Wellness:** You can enjoy the saving on vitamins, supplements, sport wears and products, and health related services.
- **Travel and Leisure:** Hurry and book your next vacation at great prices for flights, hotels, car rentals, and travel bundles.
- **Food and Groceries:** Cut costs on your grocery bills with coupons for food, beverages, and household essentials.

## How to Use Coupons and Discount Codes:

1. **Browse and Select:** Browse our extensive list of today’s top coupons and discount codes. Select the one that best suits your needs.
2. **Copy the Code:** Click the button coupon or discount code to show the code. right-click the link to copy it to the clipboard.
3. **Shop and Save:** Once on the retailer’s website, you can get to the products of your choice, select them and add them to the cart Then you can enter the code at the time of payment to get your discount.

### Benefits of Using Our Coupons and Discount Codes:

- **Verified Codes:** We also make sure that all our coupons and the discount codes that we use are valid and offer the best possible savings.
- **Wide Range of Stores:** Many codes are presented to you: various stores and brands are available on our site.
- **Daily Updates:** This list of coupons and discount codes changes every day, providing our customers with the new information.
- **User-Friendly:** Therefore, our interface was created to ensure that coupons are easy to find and that their usage does not take a lot of effort.

3. **Check the App for Promo Offers**


Uber Eats regularly features deals in its “Promotions” section. Simply go to your profile in the app, tap on “Promotions,” and discover current coupon codes, exclusive discounts, and special offers on select restaurants or order types. Many deals are automatically added to your account, making them easy to use.

Stay in the loop by following Uber Eats on platforms like [Instagram](https://www.instagram.com/ubereats/?hl=en), [Facebook](https://www.facebook.com/UberEats/), and [X](https://twitter.com/UberEats?ref_src=twsrc%5Egoogle%7Ctwcamp%5Eserp%7Ctwgr%5Eauthor) (formerly Twitter). 5. **Enjoy More Benefits with Uber One**


Frequent user? Subscribing to Uber One unlocks perks like unlimited $0 delivery fees, exclusive member-only promotions, up to 10% off eligible food and grocery orders, discounted rides, and priority support—all for a monthly fee. Regular use can lead to substantial savings both on food delivery and transportation.
6. **Find Discounts on Uber Eats Gift Cards**


Look for Uber Eats gift cards sold at a discount from third-party retailers. Buying gift cards below face value means extra savings every time you order, and these funds typically never expire. This simple hack adds more value to your next meal—even when you don’t have a promo code at hand.

### How to Use an Uber Eats Coupon Code

1. Open the Uber Eats [app](https://about.ubereats.com/us/en/how-it-works/download-uber-eats-app/) or [website](https://www.ubereats.com/) and sign in.
2. Browse for your favorite food and add items to your cart.
3. When you’re ready, go to your cart and look for the “Add Promo Code” option.
4. Enter your coupon code and hit apply. You’ll see the discount pop up if the code works.
5. Double-check your total to make sure the discount has been applied.

### More Ways to Save at Uber Eats (Hacks & Tricks)

1. **Check the Offers Tab for Fresh Discounts:**


Uber Eats regularly refreshes its “Offers” section with new deals. Before you place an order, check this tab to find exclusive restaurant promotions, free delivery options, or percentage-off discounts tailored to your area.
2. **Order During Off-Peak Times for Extra Savings:**


Restaurants and Uber Eats often run better deals or lower delivery fees in non-peak hours like late afternoons or mid-mornings. Scheduling your orders during these windows can help you tap into specials that aren’t available during peak times.
3. **Seek Out Bundle Combos:**


Many restaurants feature bundle deals on Uber Eats—meal combos or family packs that give you more for less compared to ordering items separately. These are ideal for groups or gatherings and can stretch your rupee further.
4. **Group Your Orders to Cut Delivery Fees:**


If you’re ordering with friends or family, place a combined order rather than separate ones. This lets you split delivery fees, helping everyone save without compromising on individual preferences.
5. **Use Cashback Apps and Portals for Every Order:**


Increase your savings by placing Uber Eats orders through cashback platforms like TopCashback, ShopBack, or Cheddar. You can earn up to 5% instant cashback by buying Uber Eats gift cards on Cheddar, or get fixed-amount cashback on orders via TopCashback or ShopBack. Always check for exclusions—such as promo code compatibility—before ordering.
6. **Maximize Credit Card Benefits:**


Paying with a credit card that offers cashback or rewards for dining and online purchases can further reduce your effective spend. Some cards provide special Uber Eats promotions, monthly credits, or increased points that stack with other deals.

Ordering your favorite meals with Uber Eats is incredibly easy and fast. Simply open the Uber Eats app or visit their website, browse through menus from local restaurants, and place your order with just a few taps. If you need your food quickly, Uber Eats is known for speedy deliveries, often arriving within 30 minutes. Additionally, you can schedule deliveries up to a week in advance, choosing a specific time in 30-minute increments—ideal for planning meals during lunch breaks, meetings, or family dinners. ### About Uber Eats

Uber Eats is a leading global online food ordering and delivery platform launched by Uber in 2014. As of 2025, it operates in more than **12,000 cities across over 50 countries**, partnering with over **1 million restaurants** worldwide to bring a wide range of cuisines directly to customers’ doors.

The platform allows users to browse menus, place orders via the app or website, and track deliveries in real time. Uber Eats employs a network of couriers who use cars, bikes, or scooters to deliver meals quickly—often within 30 minutes—and also offers scheduled deliveries for planned meals.

## Frequently Asked Questions

### What is Uber Eats?

Uber Eats is a global online food delivery platform that allows users to order meals from a wide range of local restaurants and have them delivered quickly to their door, often within 30 minutes.

### How do I place an order on Uber Eats?

Download the Uber Eats app or visit the website. Browse menus from participating restaurants, select your desired dishes, add them to your cart, and complete payment at checkout. You can then track your order’s progress in real time.

### Which areas does Uber Eats serve?

Uber Eats is available in more than 12,000 cities across over 50 countries as of 2025.

### How does delivery tracking work?

Once you place your order, the app lets you follow each stage: from meal preparation to the delivery partner picking up your food and completing the delivery to your location. You’ll see the estimated delivery time and live updates.

### Can I schedule a delivery for later?

Yes, Uber Eats allows you to schedule orders in advance. Choose your delivery time during checkout to have meals arrive exactly when you need them.

### Are there pick-up options available?

In select cities, Uber Eats offers pick-up and dine-in options. You place your order through the app and collect it directly from the restaurant when it’s ready.

## About Dominos

Dominos Pizza, established in 1960 and headquartered in Michigan, USA, stands as a global leader in pizza delivery. With over 10,000 franchised and corporate outlets worldwide, it ranks as the second-largest pizza chain globally. Entering the Indian market in 1996, Dominos has captured over 54% market share and dominates 70% of the home delivery pizza segment.

Known for its commitment to quality, Dominos uses freshly cut vegetables and premium ingredients to craft mouthwatering pizzas, sandwiches, toppings, and more. Don’t miss out on incredible savings with Dominos Wednesday Deals, Freaky Friday Offers, Weekend Specials, and exclusive discount codes!

## FAQs

### Q1: What is the Domino’s 6 in 1 Pizza?

The Domino’s Big Big 6-in-1 Pizza is a large, square pizza with 24 slices, featuring six distinct flavors in a single order—perfect for groups and parties. It’s designed to offer variety and solve topping debates for big gatherings.

### Q2: How big is the 6 in 1 pizza from Domino’s?

The 6-in-1 Pizza is a massive, square-shaped pizza, designed for sharing, and comes with 24 slices—larger than standard Domino’s pizzas.

### Q3: What are the prices for the Big Big 6 in 1 Pizza at Domino’s?

- Vegetarian variant: ₹799 (introductory offer)
- Non-vegetarian variant: ₹899 (introductory offer)

These prices are available at Domino’s stores and on the Domino’s app in India.

### Q4: Can I get a Domino’s coupon code for 50% off today?

Yes, Domino’s frequently offers limited-time coupon codes such as 50% off on pizzas, through their app or website. Codes and offers may change, so check the Domino’s app or trusted coupon sites for today’s active deal.

### Q5: Is there a Domino’s coupon code for ₹100 off today?

Domino’s India often runs flat ₹100 off coupons for specific order values. Visit the Domino’s website or app during checkout to see available codes, or check reputable coupon sources for real-time offers.

### Q6: What is Domino’s Volcano Pizza or Cheese Volcano Pizza?

Domino’s Volcano Pizza (including Cheese Volcano) is a menu item known for its stuffed, molten cheese-filled crust or “cheese volcano” design, offering an extra-cheesy, indulgent experience. Regional availability may vary.

### Q7: What are the prices for Domino’s Volcano Pizza/Cheese Volcano?

The exact price can vary by location. To see the current price for Cheese Volcano or Volcano Pizza, check the Domino’s menu online or app, as new specialty pizzas may be priced higher than standard options.

### Q8: Where can I order the 6 in 1 pizza or Cheese Volcano pizza?

You can order these specialty pizzas through any Domino’s outlet or directly on the Domino’s app or website, whenever they are in stock and available in your city.

### Q9: What is the ‘Big Big 6 in 1 Pizza’ best for?

It’s ideal for celebrations such as watch parties, birthdays, and group hangouts—any time you want a mix of flavors and lots of pizza to share.

### Q10: Are there additional Domino’s deals available today?

Apart from 50% off and ₹100 off coupons, Domino’s is running the “Best Deal Ever” in the US, letting customers get any pizza with any toppings for $9.99 when ordered online until August 3, 2025.

## What Are McDonald’s Coupons?

McDonald’s coupons are discounts or promotional offers that reduce the price of specific items or combos on the menu. These can come in several forms: digital codes, in-app offers, paper coupons, or special daily deals. Some give you a free item with purchase, others offer dollar-off discounts, and many offer buy-one-get-one-free (BOGO) deals.

You can find these deals through various channels, including the McDonald’s app, official website, third-party coupon sites, and even local flyers.

## Why Use McDonald’s Coupons?

Fast food is all about convenience and value — and McDonald’s coupons take that value even further. Here’s why using them makes sense:

- Save money on your favorite meals.

- Try new menu items without paying full price.

- Feed the family with bundle deals at a lower cost.

- Get exclusive in-app rewards for frequent visits.


If you eat at McDonald’s regularly or even just occasionally, using coupons helps you stretch your budget while enjoying the food you love.

## How to Find the Best McDonald’s Coupons

Wondering how to track down current deals? Here are the best methods:

1. Download the McDonald’s App – This is the most reliable source for updated coupons. The app offers personalized deals based on your order history and location. You can even place your order directly through the app and apply your deal at checkout.

2. Check the McDonald’s Website – Some regional McDonald’s locations list local promotions online. Visit the “Deals” or “Offers” section on the site for updated savings.

3. Look for Email Promotions – If you sign up for McDonald’s emails, you’ll receive news, updates, and exclusive discounts straight to your inbox.

4. Visit Coupon Websites – Trusted coupon platforms often feature verified McDonald’s coupons. Be sure to find a reputable site with active and regularly updated listings.

5. Ask at Your Local McDonald’s – Some franchises offer local promotions not advertised online. It doesn’t hurt to ask at the counter or drive-thru!


### What Kinds of Deals Can You Expect?

Depending on the season or location, here’s what you might be able to find:

- BOGO breakfast sandwiches

- Free fries with app download

- $1 drinks during summer

- $2 off a combo meal

- McDelivery deals via DoorDash or Uber Eats


Deals rotate frequently, especially around holidays, game days, and product launches. Staying updated through the app ensures you never miss a limited-time offer.

### Recommendations to Maximize Your Savings

Here are a few smart tips and recommendations to help you get more out of every McDonald’s coupon:

- Stack deals when possible. Some offers can be used together, like an in-app reward plus a paper coupon.

- Order through the app to unlock exclusive mobile-only deals.

- Check daily deals. McDonald’s sometimes offers a different discount each day of the week.

- Use loyalty points. The McDonald’s Rewards program lets you earn points toward free items with every eligible purchase.


### Final Thoughts – The Best Way to Enjoy More for Less

Using McDonald’s coupons is one of the best ways to get your favorite meals for less. From breakfast bites to late-night snacks, you’ll always find something delicious at a better price when you take advantage of current offers.

## Puma Upcoming Sale

### Puma’s Biannual Clearance Sale

**Twice A Year** Clearing the shelves for new products, Puma presents a spectacular Clearance Sale with massive discounts on your favorite apparel, shoes, and accessories. This clearance event benefits customers by offering high-quality sportswear at heavily reduced prices, while also aligning perfectly with Puma’s sustainability efforts to reduce excess inventory through smart sales strategies.

As online shopping continues to gain popularity in India due to its convenience and extensive range of options, this sale provides the perfect opportunity for you to revamp your wardrobe from the comfort of your own home. Don’t miss out on this limited-time offer! Take advantage of the Puma Clearance Sale before it’s too late.

### Puma Men’s Shoes Sale

**Twice A Year** If you’re in search of stylish and functional footwear this season, look no further than the Puma Men’s Shoes Sale. This highly anticipated sale takes place twice a year and offers incredible deals on a wide range of footwear. With discounts of up to 50% off on selected collections, you can indulge in fashionable shoes without breaking the bank.

What makes this sale even more enticing are the convenient features it offers. Enjoy the flexibility of Cash on Delivery, hassle-free returns, and additional discounts for prepaid orders. Puma is dedicated to providing affordable, high-quality products, ensuring that you get excellent value for your money.

Don’t let this sale slip away! These attractive deals are too good to be overlooked. Step into trend-setting styles that enhance every stride and make a statement wherever you go. Mark your calendars and prepare to elevate your shoe game with the Puma Men’s Shoes Sale.

### Puma Mother’s Day Sale

11th to 14th May 2025

Each year, Puma celebrates Mother’s Day with a special sale featuring significant discounts across its collections, plus an extra 20% off on selected items. This sale is the perfect opportunity to honor the unique bond with your mother by treating her to exclusive products.

The offerings include a variety of athletic and casual wear that beautifully combines style, comfort, and sustainability. This Mother’s Day promotion is more than just a shopping event; it’s about creating lasting memories.

### Puma 15% Off Deal

16th to 25th May 2025

Don’t miss out on the Puma 15% Off Deal, available until May 10th! Enjoy a flat 15% discount on clothing priced above Rs. 1999 and footwear priced above Rs. 3,999. Whether you’re looking for stylish apparel or trendy footwear, this sale has everything you need.

Seize the opportunity to upgrade your wardrobe with high-quality Puma products at unbeatable prices. Hurry and shop now to take full advantage of this limited-time offer!

### Puma Fresh Drip Deal

1st to 16th August 2025

The Puma Fresh Drip Deal is happening from August 1st to 16th, offering a 40% discount on select styles for men, women, and kids. This sale features footwear, apparel, and accessories, making it a fantastic opportunity to update your wardrobe with Puma’s latest designs at reduced prices.

Explore the stylish options available and enjoy significant savings before the sale comes to an end!

### Puma Festive Sale

14th to 22nd October 2025

Puma is thrilled to announce the FEELIN FESTIVE sale, running from October 14th to 22nd! During this exciting event, you can enjoy discounts of up to 50% on a wide range of products, including footwear, apparel, and accessories for women, men, and kids.

As a bonus, you’ll receive an extra 20% off on best-selling items. This is a wonderful chance to refresh your wardrobe with the latest styles. Don’t let this opportunity pass you by—explore the sale today and snag your favorite Puma products at incredible prices!

### Puma Diwali Sale

21st to 27th October 2025

The Puma Diwali Sale in India is here, bringing exciting deals and offers across a wide range of products! This festive season, shoppers can look forward to significant discounts on Puma’s diverse collection, including athletic wear, footwear, and accessories.

Take advantage of this fantastic opportunity to enjoy savings of up to 50% or more. Whether you’re in search of sporty tracksuits or stylish dress shoes, this sale has something for everyone. Be sure to explore the full range of options available for both men and women and don’t miss out on the chance to snag the best deals this festive season!

### Puma Big Leap Sale

**Multiple Occasions**

If you’re looking to score Puma products at fantastic prices, be sure to check out the Puma Big Leap sale! This sale occurs a few times a year and offers substantial discounts, often reaching up to 50% off on a variety of Puma items, including shoes, clothing, and accessories.

Remember, this sale is available for a limited time only, so it’s wise to visit the Puma website or your local stores to find the current dates and details of the promotion. Don’t miss your chance to grab some great deals!

### Puma Black Friday Sale

29th November to 5th December 2025

Puma’s Black Friday Sale is now live, taking place from November 29th to December 5th! Enjoy discounts of 40% to 50% off on select styles of footwear, sportswear, and accessories.

Whether you’re in search of performance gear, stylish sneakers, or comfortable athleisure, this sale has something for everyone. Don’t miss your chance to snag premium Puma products at discounted prices. With these limited-time offers, it’s the perfect opportunity to refresh your sportswear collection or find a special gift for someone.

### Puma Warehouse Sale

4th October to 8th October

Get ready to uncover amazing discounts and unbeatable deals at the eagerly awaited Puma Warehouse Sale. This sale is a golden opportunity to find high-quality products at incredibly low prices, with savings of up to 70% on selected collections.

Puma understands the importance of making your favorite styles readily available, ensuring that you have an exceptional shopping experience. From trendy sneakers to stylish apparel and accessories, you’ll find everything you need to upgrade your wardrobe at this sale.

Rest assured that Puma’s advanced shipping processes guarantee timely deliveries without compromising on product quality or packaging standards. Every purchase you make during the Warehouse Sale is guaranteed to be worthwhile.

### Puma Christmas Sale

25th to 27th December

This festive season, get ready for a shopping extravaganza as Puma unveils its Christmas sale, offering consumers significant markdowns on premium sportswear that perfectly blends style and performance. The Christmas sale collection features a variety of Holiday Workout Gear designed for fitness enthusiasts who are committed to their routine, even during the chilly holiday season.

Don’t forget to explore Puma’s Winter Essentials range, which includes cozy yet stylish options like sweatshirts, hoodies, and jackets. These pieces provide warmth in harsh weather while keeping you on-trend.

## How to Buy Recommended Baby Strollers

To buy a recommended baby stroller, start by considering your lifestyle needs (urban vs. suburban, frequent travel, etc.). Look for strollers with high safety ratings, adjustable seating, and strong durability. Popular types include full-size, lightweight, jogger, and travel system strollers. Check reviews from trusted sources like Consumer Reports or parenting blogs. Test the stroller in-store for ease of use, folding mechanism, and maneuverability. Finally, compare prices online to find the best deal and ensure it includes a warranty or return policy.

### How to Choose the Best Shoe Rack Online in 2025

Selecting the right shoe rack is about more than just price. Here’s how to make the smartest choice:

- **Measure Your Space:** Always check your available area before purchasing. A shoe rack should fit comfortably without overcrowding your room.
- **Consider Material:** Choose from sturdy options like metal, solid wood, or engineered wood for long-lasting use. The material affects both durability and visual appeal.
- **Capacity Matters:** Make sure the rack can accommodate your current collection—and a few future additions. Opt for a rack with more capacity than you currently need.
- **Design & Accessibility:** Decide between open racks (for daily use and ventilation) and closed cabinets (to keep dust away and maintain a tidy look).
- **Ease of Assembly:** Check if the rack is easy to assemble and comes with all necessary tools.
- **Budget-Friendly:** Set a budget and compare options to find the best value without compromising on quality.

### Best Features to Look for in Top Shoe Racks

When you Buy Top 5 Shoe rack online At Best Price in 2025, prioritize these features:

- Multi-tier or stackable design for maximum storage in minimal space
- Adjustable or removable shelves for different shoe types, including boots and heels
- Dustproof covers or closed cabinets for cleaner storage
- Stylish finishes that match your home décor

# The 10 Best Sneaker Brands for New Runners in 2025

By [Nik](https://neudeals.co/blog/author/nik/ "Posts by Nik")July 24, 2024May 29th, 2025[No Comments](https://neudeals.co/blog/best-sneaker-brands-of-the-year/#respond)6 min read

﻿

## Discover the Best Sneaker Brands for Ultimate Comfort and Fashion

As we pass the midpoint of the year, it’s the perfect time to assess the standout sneakers of 2025. This year, the sneaker scene has seen a shift, moving away from the usual crowd-pleasers like flashy viral trends, standard retro releases, and perennial hype beast favorites. What’s capturing attention now are sneakers that showcase original design, premium materials, and clean aesthetics.

Table of Contents

[Toggle](https://neudeals.co/blog/best-sneaker-brands-of-the-year/#)

High-fashion collaborations are more vibrant than ever, with top design houses partnering with sneaker brands to reinvent classic models. New shapes and innovative materials are making a significant impact, signaling a fresh direction in sneaker culture. As traditional elements start to fade, exciting new alternatives are emerging.

Sneakers as we know them are evolving. Check out the top sneakers of 2025 at Neu Deals.

## List Of The Top 10 Sneaker Brands

### 1\. Air Jordan 1 1985

![air jordan](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20267%20300'%2F%3E)

When it comes to iconic sneakers, the 1985 Air Jordan 1 truly deserves its year in the name. The quality and construction of the 1985 Jordan are far superior to those of modern releases, and despite being 36 years old, this shoe remains remarkably wearable—a rarity for its era.

Before Michael Jordan’s documentary “The Last Dance,” the market for 1985 Air Jordan 1s was relatively niche, with prices hovering around $1,000. However, the documentary skyrocketed their value, making it nearly impossible to find a pair for under $1,500 today.

What makes this sneaker stand out at number one is its legendary story. Each time Michael Jordan wore his Jordan 1s on the court, the NBA fined him $5,000. Nike responded by covering the fines, as long as Jordan continued to wear the shoes. This commitment to a single pair of sneakers endeared them to fans and secured their place at the top of our list.

Buy Now

### 2\. Nike Air Force 1

![nike air force](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20214%20300'%2F%3E)

The term “iconic” is often used, but when it comes to truly earning the title, there’s only one shoe that fits: the Air Force 1.

Available in five distinct variations:

- Superlow
- Low
- Mid
- High
- Super high

Regardless of the style, an all-white Air Force 1 is often seen as a blank canvas. It holds the record as the most customized shoe of all time and is central to numerous notable Nike collaborations.

Buy Now

### 3\. Nike Dunk

![Nike Dunk](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20225%20300'%2F%3E)

First released in 1985 as a basketball shoe, the Dunk SB was conceived by designer Peter Moore as an evolution of the Air Force 1. This evolution marked the beginning of what is now regarded as a cornerstone of sneaker design.

Today, the Dunk craze is making a strong comeback. The market is abuzz with both classic colorways and exciting new designs. Nike SB Dunk sneakers are at an all-time high, and this trend shows no signs of slowing down.

Buy Now

### 4\. Nike Air Yeezy 1

![Nike Air Yeezy 1](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20300%20151'%2F%3E)

In 2009, Kanye West achieved a major milestone when Nike offered him a collaboration, marking a transformative moment in how artists partner with major brands.

The Air Yeezy 1 was so limited at launch that even Kanye himself struggled to secure a pair. Nike’s decision not to offer him the same royalties given to athletes ultimately led to a pivotal shift.

The highly anticipated Air Yeezy 2 Red October became a sneaker legend. Its release was repeatedly delayed, and when it finally launched, Kanye revealed that he was parting ways with Nike, leaving a lasting impact on the sneaker world.

Buy Now

### 5\. Nike Air Max 1

![Nike Air Max 1](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20267%20300'%2F%3E)

The Air Max 1, introduced in 1987, was the breakthrough that launched Tinker Hatfield’s career. Originally designed as a running shoe, it quickly evolved into an iconic lifestyle sneaker.

In the early 2000s, the Air Max 1 became the centerpiece of several legendary collaborations with brands like Clot, Patta, and Kidrobot. Today, securing a pair from these influential releases could set you back over $3,000. While you might have once wanted to wear them, they’ve now achieved classic status, making them more likely to be showcased as prized collector’s items.

Buy Now

### 6\. Adidas Yeezy 350 V1

![Adidas Yeezy 350 V1](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20300%20188'%2F%3E)

Kanye West’s impact on fashion is undeniable, but his debut 350 sneakers in 2015 took everyone by surprise. Although some may disagree, this release marked a significant boost in the replica footwear market.

The design’s simplicity might have seemed straightforward to imitators, especially given the staggering profit margins when they first launched—prices started at no less than $1,500 per pair. Even now, counterfeit versions and non-existent colorways continue to flood the market.

Buy Now

### 7\. Air Jordan III 1988

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20300%20300'%2F%3E)

The sneaker industry underwent a seismic shift, not solely due to the Air Jordan III, but thanks to Tinker Hatfield. Often hailed as the father of sneaker design, Hatfield’s innovative silhouettes revolutionized the field. The Air Jordan III’s introduction of the iconic elephant print caused a frenzy, marking a turning point that reshaped the industry forever.

Buy Now

### 8\. Adidas Superstar Pro-Model

![Adidas Superstar Pro-Model](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20250%20300'%2F%3E)

Originally designed as a professional basketball sneaker in 1970, this Adidas shoe unexpectedly transcended its sport origins to become a staple in the hip-hop dance scene.

The 1980s saw Run-D.M.C. embrace the iconic silhouette, forever changing its trajectory. This groundbreaking collaboration marked the first time a non-athlete was signed by a sneaker brand, setting the stage for future artist partnerships. Many consider this crossover as the moment sneakers became a key element in African-American culture.

Buy Now

### 9\. Converse Chuck Taylor All Star

![Converse Chuck Taylor All Star](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20250%20300'%2F%3E)

No discussion about iconic footwear is complete without mentioning the Chuck Taylor All Star. For over 113 years, Converse has been a must-have in the closets of sneaker enthusiasts everywhere.

Converse first introduced its basketball shoe in 1917, and it wasn’t until many years later that they faced serious competition in the basketball market. Over time, the Chuck Taylor evolved from a court classic to a staple in street style, earning its place in the fashion industry as a timeless icon.

Buy Now

### 10\. Adidas Basketball Forum 1984

![Adidas Basketball Forum 1984](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20300%20230'%2F%3E)

Kicking off our list is the Adidas Forum, originally launched in 1984 as a revolutionary basketball shoe. Available in both high and low-top versions, it features an adjustable velcro strap—a groundbreaking design choice for its time. While the bright blue colorway is iconic, the Forum was available in nearly every color imaginable.

The reason the Adidas Forum claims the number 10 spot on our list is due to its significant historical impact. While Nike often dominates sneaker conversations, it’s important to recognize that Adidas played a crucial role in driving innovation in the sneaker industry.

Buy Now

### How to Choose the Best Sneaker Brands for New Runners

Selecting the right sneaker brand involves considering a few key factors:

- **Cushioning:** Look for brands that offer shoes with ample cushioning to absorb impact and protect your joints.
- **Fit and Comfort:** Brands known for accommodating a variety of foot shapes and sizes help ensure a comfortable run.
- **Durability:** New runners benefit from sneakers that withstand regular use without quickly wearing out.
- **Value:** Affordable options and frequent sales from reputable brands can make starting out less daunting.
- **Reputation:** Brands with a proven track record in running shoes are more likely to deliver consistent quality.

### Best Picks: What Do Experts Recommend for New Runners?

- **Asics Novablast 5:** Frequently recommended as the top choice for beginners due to its excellent cushioning, versatility, and value.
- **Nike Pegasus:** A long-standing favorite for its balanced ride and reliability.

# Top 7 Perfume Brands for Men in India

By [Nik](https://neudeals.co/blog/author/nik/ "Posts by Nik")December 2, 2024May 30th, 2025[No Comments](https://neudeals.co/blog/top-7-perfume-brands-for-men-in-india/#respond)5 min read

## Top 7 Best Perfume Brands for Men in India: Expert Recommendations!

Fragrance has always been a key element in personal style. In India, its use dates back thousands of years to the Indus Civilization. Finding the perfect scent that resonates with you is essential. Interested in discovering the leading perfume brands for men in India? Before we dive in, let’s explore the world of fragrances a bit more. Fragrances are generally classified into six to seven families, including citrus, green, musky, woody, spicy, and floral, among others. Understanding scent notes is crucial when selecting your signature fragrance. Scent notes are divided into three primary categories: top, heart, and base notes. These components significantly influence the fragrance’s development and longevity. In this blog, we’ll guide you through the top perfume brands for men that are favored by Indian consumers.

Table of Contents

[Toggle](https://neudeals.co/blog/top-7-perfume-brands-for-men-in-india/#)

The way various perfume notes blend with your skin plays a vital role in defining your unique scent. To help you identify your preferred fragrance, the fragrance wheel can be an excellent resource.  Best Trending Perfume like: Versace dylan blue, Jean paul gaultier le male, jean paul gaultier ultra male, armani cologne, tom ford perfume, jean paul gaultier cologne, versace eros edp, guess seductive noir, narciso rodriguez for him, the most wanted azzaro, versace eros 100ml, etc. find more offers on Neudeals.

## The List of The Top 7 Perfume Companies

### 1\. Beardo

Set sail on a journey of sophistication and adventure with Mariner Perfume EDP. This Eau de Parfum captures the essence of the open sea, blending fresh, aquatic notes with a hint of mystery to create a scent that’s both invigorating and refined. Ideal for the modern man who seeks a fragrance that embodies freedom, strength, and elegance, Mariner Perfume is perfect for daily wear or special occasions. Command attention with the bold and charismatic GodFather Perfume. This powerful fragrance is crafted for men who exude confidence and authority. With its rich, intense scent, GodFather Perfume is perfect for making a strong statement, whether at work, a formal event, or a night out.

### 2\. The Man Company

![Top 7 Perfume Brands for Men in India](https://neudeals.co/wp-content/uploads/2024/12/50-night-long-lasting-perfume-the-man-company-men-original-imah45hgjbxh43hk-300x277.jpeg)

Eau De Parfum Night by The Man Company is an enigmatic concoction of citrusy, exotic and woody notes. It is a perfect pick for a man who has much more to his personality than he showcases to those around him. He has a subtle charm that makes people want to know more about him. At the first hit of Night, you witness the tangy punch of Grapefruit, followed by the exotic touch of White Flowers, and at the base lies the woody Amber.

### 3\. Wow Skin Science

Buy at Flipkart

Wow Skin Science is a rising brand in the men’s grooming sector, offering an extensive selection of products for skin, hair, body, and beard care, as well as affordable men’s fragrances. You can find your ideal scent from their diverse collection of perfumes. Available both in stores and online, Wow Skin Science has successfully penetrated both markets. Their perfumes are known for their long-lasting qualities, making them an excellent choice for any occasion. Crafted with base notes that enhance charm and sophistication, some of the popular fragrances from Wow Skin Science include Gulmarg Mist, Beach Walk, Doon Deodar, and Ocean Waves.

### 4\. Skinn By Titan

Skinn features a blend of refreshing and invigorating notes, ensuring a luxurious fragrance experience that lasts throughout the day. This Indian brand combines local craftsmanship with global expertise to create exceptional scents. Explore the enticing fragrances designed by experts at Skinn by Titan, which are sure to be admired by all. With options that cater to both modern and traditional tastes, you can find the perfect scent from their extensive collection. As a versatile brand, they offer timeless notes that appeal to everyone. You can find these perfumes at competitive prices on online shopping sites like Amazon and Flipkart. Among Skinn’s popular fragrances for men are Amalfi Bleu, Steele, and Raw.

### 5\. Denver

Buy at Flipkart

Denver stands out as a premier and esteemed brand in men’s grooming, offering a range of products including fragrances, skincare, haircare, body care, and beard essentials. Their long-lasting perfumes embody the essence of a true gentleman. You can choose Denver’s luxurious fragrances as a staple in your daily routine and enjoy a touch of timeless elegance. Additionally, you can find discounts on your favorite Denver perfumes on Amazon and Flipkart. Denver also offers premium luxury gift sets for men, adding a personalized touch to your gifting at accessible prices. Some of Denver’s popular fragrances include Denver Hamilton, Utopia, Arch, Insight, and Blackcode.

### 6\. Ajmal Perfumes

Buy at Flipkart

Ajmal Perfumes is a prominent player in the fragrance industry, boasting a diverse collection of over 300 enchanting scents. Renowned globally for its high-quality offerings at reasonable prices, Ajmal has established itself as one of the leading brands for men’s fragrances, proudly crafting enduring perfumes across three generations. The company excels in the art of perfumery, thanks to the expertise of its dedicated team. With a commitment to innovation, integrity, and fair business practices, Ajmal continues to thrive. Among their most sought-after men’s fragrances are Oudh Smear, Mukhallat Al Wafa, and Summer Sage.

### How to Choose the Best Perfume Brand for You

Selecting the right perfume is about matching your personality, lifestyle, and the Indian climate. Here’s how to make the best choice:

- **Understand Your Preferences:** Do you like woody, citrus, floral, or spicy scents? For example, Ajmal offers bold oud, while Skinn by Titan delivers fresh, modern notes.
- **Consider Longevity:** Look for brands known for long-lasting fragrances, such as Laurelle London and French Essence.
- **Occasion Matters:** Choose lighter, fresher scents for daily and office wear (like Skinn by Titan or French Essence), and richer, more intense perfumes for evenings and special events (like Tom Ford or Creed).
- **Budget:** Indian brands like Skinn and Ajmal offer quality at lower prices, while international luxury brands come at a premium.

### Recommended Picks for Every Indian Man

- **For Everyday Freshness:** Skinn by Titan and French Essence are perfect for daily use and the Indian climate.
- **For Special Occasions:** Tom Ford Oud Wood and Creed Aventus make a statement at any event.
- **For Affordable Luxury:** Ajmal Perfumes and Armaf offer high-quality scents without breaking the bank.
- **For Long-lasting Impact:** Laurelle London and Dior Sauvage are renowned for their staying power and sophistication.

### FAQs

#### When is the next Flipkart sale?

The upcoming Flipkart sale, the Monumental Sale 2025, begins on January 14th. However, Flipkart Plus customers can enjoy early access starting a day before.

#### Will there be any bank offers or cashback during the Flipkart sale 2025?

Yes, Flipkart typically features bank offers during major sales, offering up to 10% instant discounts when using partner bank cards.

#### How can I score the best deals during the Flipkart sale 2025?

To secure the best deals, make sure to use the right bank card to maximize your discount. Additionally, it’s crucial to shop early, as popular deals may sell out quickly, causing prices to rise.

### FAQs

#### Q. When is the next Nykaa sale?

A. Nykaa typically announces its upcoming sales a few weeks in advance. Keep an eye on their official website or app for updates on the next sale dates.

#### Q. **What discounts can I expect during Nykaa’s upcoming sales?**

A. Nykaa sales often feature discounts of up to 50% or more on beauty, skincare, and wellness products. Specific offers may vary by brand and category.

#### Q. **Which brands participate in Nykaa sales?**

A. Top brands like Maybelline, L’Oréal, MAC, Lakmé, and many others participate in Nykaa sales, offering exclusive deals and discounts.

#### Q. **How can I stay informed about Nykaa’s upcoming sales?**

A. You can subscribe to Nykaa’s newsletter, follow them on social media, or download the Nykaa app to receive notifications about upcoming sales and special offers.

# क्या रैपिडो ज़ोमैटो-स्विगी की एकाधिकार को तोड़ सकता है?

By [Nik](https://neudeals.co/blog/author/nik/ "Posts by Nik")June 10, 2025June 30th, 2025[No Comments](https://neudeals.co/blog/can-rapido-break-the-zomato-swiggy-duopoly/#respond)1 min read

दोपहिया वाहन राइड-हेलिंग कंपनी रैपिडो, प्रतिस्पर्धी फ़ूड डिलीवरी बाज़ार में प्रवेश कर रही है, जिसके पास पर्याप्त फंडिंग है और अपनी पहुँच का विस्तार करने की योजना है। ज़ोमैटो-स्विगी के एकाधिकार को बाधित करने के उद्देश्य से, रैपिडो रेस्तराँ को कम कमीशन दरें प्रदान करता है। हालाँकि, कंपनी को उच्च परिचालन लागत और एक मांग वाले उद्योग में खुद को अलग करने की आवश्यकता सहित चुनौतियों का सामना करना पड़ रहा है।

Table of Contents

[Toggle](https://neudeals.co/blog/can-rapido-break-the-zomato-swiggy-duopoly/#)

## फ़ूड डिलीवरी में रैपिडो के प्रवेश का बाज़ार के लिए क्या मतलब है?

भारत के फ़ूड डिलीवरी परिदृश्य पर लंबे समय से ज़ोमैटो\-स्विगी की एकाधिकार का दबदबा रहा है। अब, रैपिडो\- जो अपनी बाइक\-टैक्सी सेवा के लिए जाना जाता है\- फ़ूड डिलीवरी में अपने साहसिक कदम के साथ सुर्खियाँ बटोर रहा है। लेकिन इसका रेस्तराँ, ग्राहकों और पूरे उद्योग के लिए क्या मतलब है? आइए इसे समझते हैं।

## रैपिडो ज़ोमैटो-स्विगी की Duopoly को कैसे तोड़ रहा है?

रैपिडो एक सरल, विघटनकारी रणनीति के साथ बाज़ार में हलचल मचा रहा है: रेस्तराँ के लिए कम कमीशन। जबकि ज़ोमैटो और स्विगी आम तौर पर 16-30% कमीशन लेते हैं, रैपिडो का मॉडल सिर्फ़ 8-15% तक है। रेस्तराँ के लिए, इसका मतलब है अधिक टिकाऊ मार्जिन और मेन्यू की कीमतें बढ़ाने का कम दबाव। ग्राहकों के लिए, रैपिडो की निश्चित डिलीवरी फीस – ₹400 से कम के ऑर्डर के लिए ₹25, और अधिक ऑर्डर के लिए ₹50 – बेहतर डील और अधिक विकल्प प्रदान कर सकती है।

रैपिडो अपने मौजूदा 4 मिलियन बाइक बेड़े का भी लाभ उठा रहा है, टैक्सी और डिलीवरी जॉब के बीच राइडर्स का क्रॉस-यूटिलाइज़ेशन कर रहा है ताकि दक्षता को अधिकतम किया जा सके और लागत कम रखी जा सके। यह दृष्टिकोण रैपिडो को लगभग शून्य अतिरिक्त पूंजी निवेश के साथ खाद्य वितरण क्षेत्र में प्रवेश करने की अनुमति देता है।

यह उच्च लागतों से जूझ रहे छोटे रेस्तराओं और बेहतर डील की तलाश कर रहे उपभोक्ताओं के लिए गेम चेंजर हो सकता है।

![rapido food delivery](https://neudeals.co/wp-content/uploads/2025/06/rapido-food-delivery-819x1024.jpg)

Source: Inc42 Research

## सबसे अच्छे कारण कि Rapido Food Delivery की रणनीति क्यों काम कर सकती है

- **रेस्तरां के लिए कम लागत:** कम कमीशन का मतलब है कि अधिक रेस्तरां प्लेटफ़ॉर्म से जुड़ सकते हैं, जिससे ग्राहकों को अधिक विविधता मिल सकती है।
- **कुशल रसद:** रैपिडो की बाइक सेना पहले से ही प्रमुख शहरों में मौजूद है, जिससे इसके बेड़े का तेजी से विस्तार और बेहतर उपयोग हो सकता है।
- **उद्योग समर्थन:** नेशनल रेस्टोरेंट एसोसिएशन ऑफ इंडिया इस क्षेत्र के लिए एक अधिक निष्पक्ष, अधिक टिकाऊ मॉडल विकसित करने के लिए रैपिडो के साथ बातचीत कर रहा है – कुछ ऐसा जो कई छोटे रेस्तरां मालिक चाहते हैं।
- **ग्राहक बचत:** पारदर्शी, निश्चित डिलीवरी शुल्क मूल्य-संवेदनशील ग्राहकों को आकर्षित कर सकता है।

## क्या **रैपिडो** Zomato-Swiggy Duopoly को तोड़ सकता है? हमारी सिफारिश

इतिहास से पता चलता है कि खाद्य वितरण में स्थापित खिलाड़ियों को चुनौती देना कठिन है – ओला और उबर के पिछले प्रयास परिचालन चुनौतियों और तीव्र प्रतिस्पर्धा के कारण विफल हो गए। हालाँकि, रैपिडो के विशाल राइडर नेटवर्क, कम कमीशन और रेस्तराँ से समर्थन का अनूठा संयोजन इसे लड़ने का मौका देता है।

### हम अनुशंसा करते हैं:

- रेस्तराँ को लागत कम करने और नए ग्राहकों तक पहुँचने के लिए रैपिडो के साथ साझेदारी करने पर विचार करना चाहिए।
- रैपिडो के विस्तार के साथ ग्राहकों को प्रतिस्पर्धी मूल्य निर्धारण और नए विकल्पों पर नज़र रखनी चाहिए।
- निवेशकों और उद्योग पर नज़र रखने वालों को बेंगलुरु में रैपिडो के पायलट पर नज़र रखनी चाहिए, क्योंकि वहाँ इसका प्रदर्शन संकेत दे सकता है कि क्या कोई वास्तविक व्यवधान क्षितिज पर है।

**संक्षेप में:** खाद्य वितरण में रैपिडो का प्रवेश परिवर्तन के लिए भूखे उद्योग के लिए ताज़ी हवा का झोंका है। हालांकि ज़ोमैटो-स्विगी की एकाधिकार को खत्म करना आसान नहीं होगा, लेकिन रैपिडो की रणनीति – अगर अच्छी तरह से क्रियान्वित की गई – अंततः रेस्तरां और ग्राहकों दोनों को एक वास्तविक विकल्प दे सकती है।

# About Us

Welcome to Neu Deals your premier destination for discovering the most current and valuable coupons and deals available in today’s market. At Neu Deals, we believe in the power of smart savings and are dedicated to helping you unlock the best offers across a diverse range of categories — from food, quick commerce, fashion and electronics to travel, groceries, and more.

## Our Vision

We envision a world where saving money is second nature. At Neu Deals, we’re transforming how people shop by making discounts an essential part of every purchase. Our goal is to become the most trusted and innovative platform in the coupons and deals space — known for our commitment to value, reliability, and customer-first thinking.

## Our Mission

Our mission is to empower shoppers with the most authentic, verified, and up-to-date deals. We strive to simplify the savings process, making it easy for you to discover, access, and apply the best available offers. Every decision we make is guided by our focus on user experience, ensuring a seamless and rewarding journey for every visitor.

## Driven by Quality and Innovation

At Neu Deals, quality is never compromised. Our user interface is designed to be intuitive and efficient, helping you find relevant deals in just a few clicks. Behind the scenes, our dedicated team works tirelessly to validate and update listings, ensuring you get access to only the most trustworthy and timely offers. Innovation fuels our growth, and we’re constantly evolving to deliver a better, smarter way to save.

## Building Strong Partnerships

Collaboration is key to our success. Through strategic B2B partnerships with top e-commerce platforms and a vast network within the tech sector, we’re able to provide exclusive deals that benefit both our users and our partners. Our business programs help brands increase visibility, drive sales, and reach wider audiences — all while delivering unmatched value to customers.

## Focused on You, the User

Everything we do starts with our users. We understand how important it is to save money without sacrificing quality. That’s why we carefully select our partners and prioritize deals that align with your needs. # Privacy policy

At Neudeals, accessible from [https://neudeals.co/](https://neudeals.co/), one of our main priorities is the privacy of our visitors. This Privacy Policy document contains types of information that is collected and recorded by Neudeals and how we use it. If you have additional questions or require more information about our Privacy Policy, do not hesitate to contact us through email at [info@neudeals.co](mailto:info@neudeals.co)

## Log Files

Neudeals follows a standard procedure of using log files. These files log visitors when they visit websites. All hosting companies do this and a part of hosting services’ analytics. The information collected by log files include internet protocol (IP) addresses, browser type, Internet Service Provider (ISP), date and time stamp, referring/exit pages, and possibly the number of clicks. These are not linked to any information that is personally identifiable. The purpose of the information is for analysing trends, administering the site, tracking users’ movement on the website, and gathering demographic information.

## Information We May Collect From You

- We may collect either your contact information, relationship information, location information, analytics information or all of them.
- We may also store a record of your correspondence when you contact us.
- We may ask your information purely for surveys that we use for research purposes, although you may choose not to participate in such surveys.

## Privacy Policies

Third-party ad servers or ad networks uses technologies like cookies, JavaScript, or Web Beacons that are used in their respective advertisements and links that appear on Neudeals, which are sent directly to users’ browser. They automatically receive your IP address when this occurs. These technologies are used to measure the effectiveness of their advertising campaigns and/or to personalize the advertising content that you see on websites that you visit. Note that Neudeals has no access to or control over these technologies that are used by third-party advertisers.

## Third Party Privacy Policies

Neudeals Privacy Policy does not apply to other advertisers or websites. Thus, we are advising you to consult the respective Privacy Policies of these third-party ad servers for more detailed information. It may include their practices and instructions about how to opt-out of certain options.

You can choose to disable cookies through your individual browser options. To know more detailed information about cookie management with specific web browsers, it can be found at the browsers’ respective websites. What Are Cookies?

## Children’s Information

Another part of our priority is adding protection for children while using the internet. We encourage parents and guardians to observe, participate in, and/or monitor and guide their online activity.

Neudeals does not knowingly collect any Personal Identifiable Information from children under the age of 13. If you think that your child provided this kind of information on our website, we strongly encourage you to contact us immediately and we will do our best efforts to promptly remove such information from our records.

## Online Privacy Policy Only

This Privacy Policy applies only to our online activities and is valid for visitors to our website with regards to the information that they shared and/or collect in Neudeals. This policy is not applicable to any information collected offline or via channels other than this website.

### Do we use ‘cookies’?

Yes. Cookies are small files that a site or its service provider transfers to your computer’s hard drive through your Web browser (if you allow) that enables the site’s or service provider’s systems to recognize your browser and capture and remember certain information. For instance, we use cookies to help us remember and process the items in your shopping cart. They are also used to help us understand your preferences based on previous or current site activity, which enables us to provide you with improved services. We also use cookies to help us compile aggregate data about site traffic and site interaction so that we can offer better site experiences and tools in the future.

#### We use cookies to:

- Understand and save user’s preferences for future visits.
- Compile aggregate data about site traffic and site interactions in order to offer better site experiences and tools in the future. We may also use trusted third-party services that track this information on our behalf.

You can choose to have your computer warn you each time a cookie is being sent, or you can choose to turn off all cookies. You do this through your browser settings. Each browser is a little different, so look at your browser’s Help menu to learn the correct way to modify your cookies.

If you disable cookies, some features will be disabled. It won’t affect the user’s experience that make your site experience more efficient and some of our services will not function properly.

### Third-Party Disclosure

We do not sell, trade, or otherwise transfer to outside parties your personally identifiable information unless we provide you with advance notice. This does not include website hosting partners and other parties who assist us in operating our website, conducting our business, or servicing you, so long as those parties agree to keep this information confidential. We may also release your information when we believe release is appropriate to comply with the law, enforce our site policies, or protect ours or others’ rights, property, or safety.

However, non-personally identifiable visitor information may be provided to other parties for marketing, advertising, or other uses.

### Third-party links

We include or offer third-party products or services on our website. These third-party sites have separate and independent privacy policies. We, therefore, have no responsibility or liability for the content and activities of these linked sites. Nonetheless, we seek to protect the integrity of our site and welcome any feedback about these sites.

## Children’s Privacy

If You are a parent or guardian and You are aware that Your child has provided Us with Personal Data, please contact Us. ## Links to Other Websites

We have no control over and assume no responsibility for the content, privacy policies or practices of any third party sites or services.

By [Nik](https://neudeals.co/blog/author/nik/ "Posts by Nik")May 2, 2025June 10th, 2025[No Comments](https://neudeals.co/blog/top-8-best-korean-skincare-brands-in-india/#respond)8 min read

## Best Korean Skincare Brands

Korean skincare isn’t just a trend—it’s a global beauty movement that’s transformed my own routine and countless others. When I first dipped my toes into K-beauty, I was skeptical. But after trying a few cult favorites, I found myself hooked on the results: hydrated, glowing skin that actually looked healthier.

Table of Contents

### COSRX: Korean Skincare Brand

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20225%20300'%2F%3E)

**COSRX**, the brand behind the viral Snail Mucin Essence. I’ll admit, the idea of snail mucin sounded odd at first, but after just a few weeks, my skin was noticeably smoother and less irritated. It’s become my go-to for calming breakouts and boosting moisture, and I’m not alone. This product has racked up thousands of rave reviews worldwide

- Targets acne, blemishes, and hyperpigmentation
- Formulated with high-quality, skin-friendly ingredients
- Known for best-sellers like Acne Pimple Master Patch and the Hydrium line
- Ideal for sensitive skin with minimal irritation
- Offers deep hydration without heaviness
- Trusted by skincare enthusiasts worldwide

**Best Selling Korean Skincare products from COSRX**

- Advanced Snail 92 All in One cream

### Laneige: The Hydration Powerhouse of Korean Skincare Product Brand

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20225%20300'%2F%3E)

**Laneige** was my gateway brand. Their Water Sleeping Mask is legendary for a reason. After a long week, I’d slather it on before bed, and by morning, my skin felt plump and refreshed—like I’d actually gotten eight hours of sleep (even when I hadn’t). Laneige’s focus on deep hydration makes it a staple for anyone battling dryness or dullness.

- Specializes in intense hydration with advanced water science
- Ideal for dry, dull, or dehydrated skin
- Known for transforming skin to be plump, smooth, and radiant
- Offers a range of products targeting moisture retention and rejuvenation
- Highly regarded worldwide for its effectiveness in hydrating and revitalizing skin

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20225%20300'%2F%3E)

For those who love eco-friendly options, **Innisfree** is inspired by the lush landscape of Jeju Island. Their Green Tea Seed Serum is lightweight but packs a punch in hydration. I took it on a trip to a dry climate, and it kept my skin dewy and comfortable the whole time.

**Main Points:**

- Offers effective skincare solutions for various skin concerns

When I first tried **The Face Shop**, I was searching for affordable Korean skincare that didn’t compromise on quality. Their Real Nature Sheet Masks quickly became my go-to for a midweek pick-me-up—each one is packed with natural ingredients and leaves my skin feeling refreshed without breaking the bank. What I love most is that The Face Shop uses over 600 natural ingredients from around the world, so I can trust I’m treating my skin gently and effectively. Whether I’m dealing with dryness or just want a healthy glow, The Face Shop’s accessible prices and wide range make it easy to build a routine that actually works for me.

### Beauty of Joeson: Best Selling Korean Skincare Brand

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20225%20300'%2F%3E)

**Beauty of Joseon** has quickly become one of my go-to Korean skincare brands, especially after I tried their Relief Sun sunscreen on a friend’s recommendation. I remember being skeptical—most sunscreens leave my skin greasy or cause breakouts—but this one felt weightless and left my face glowing, not shiny. What sets Beauty of Joseon apart is its blend of traditional Korean ingredients, like rice and ginseng, with modern science, making each product gentle yet effective. After a month of using their Dynasty Cream and Glow Serum, I noticed my skin looked brighter and felt softer, so much so that a coworker asked what I’d changed in my routine. It’s no surprise their products are bestsellers; they deliver real results without the fuss.

- Combines traditional Hanbang ingredients with modern skincare science.
- Offers clean, cruelty-free products suitable for various skin types.
- Focuses on effective formulations targeting common skin concerns.
- Inspired by the beauty rituals of the Joseon Dynasty.
- Popular among skincare enthusiasts for its quality and tradition.

**Here are the best-selling products from Beauty of Joseon:**

### Klairs: Gentle and Top Rated Korean Skincare Brand

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20225%20300'%2F%3E)

**Klairs** has earned its reputation as a gentle, top-rated Korean skincare brand by truly putting sensitive skin first. When I switched to their Supple Preparation Unscented Toner during a harsh winter, my skin finally stopped stinging and started feeling balanced again—a small miracle after years of irritation from harsher products. Klairs keeps things simple but effective, using only essential, high-quality ingredients and leaving out dyes, alcohol, and parabens. Their cruelty-free, vegan formulas mean you can care for your skin and your conscience at the same time, making Klairs a brand I trust for both results and peace of mind.

- Specializes in gentle formulations suitable for sensitive skin.
- Adheres to a “Simple but Enough” skincare philosophy.

### Dr.Jart+: A Science-Backed Korean Skincare Brand Transforming Skin Health

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20300%20224'%2F%3E)

**Dr.Jart+** stands out as a Korean skincare brand that truly puts science at the heart of every formula, blending advanced dermatological research with creative flair. I remember the first time I tried their Cicapair™ Colour Correcting Treatment—my skin was red and irritated after a long day outdoors, but within minutes, the green-to-beige cream calmed the redness and left my complexion looking naturally even. Founded by an architect inspired by a clinic-only blemish balm, Dr.Jart+ has redefined what skincare can achieve at home, making professional-level results accessible without a doctor’s visit. Their playful packaging and innovative products make every step of my routine feel like a treat, but it’s the visible, real-life results that keep me coming back.

- Combines advanced dermatological science with innovative skincare solutions.
- Offers targeted treatments for sensitivity, dryness, and uneven skin tone.
- Renowned for its Cicapair™ and Ceramidin™ product lines.
- Introduced BB Creams to the Western market, revolutionizing skincare routines.
- Provides a range of products suitable for various skin types and concerns.

### Belif: A Trusted Korean Skincare Brand Bringing Herbal Science to Everyday Beauty

![](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20225%20300'%2F%3E)

I first tried their iconic Aqua Bomb moisturizer during a harsh winter, and it was a game-changer. My skin stayed hydrated and fresh, even when the cold usually left it dry and tight. What makes Belif unique is its commitment to using natural herbs and clean ingredients, inspired by 150-year-old apothecary techniques from Scottish herbalist Duncan Napier. Each product feels gentle yet effective, free from harsh chemicals, and perfect for anyone seeking honest, herbal-powered skincare that delivers real results.

## How Do These Brands Work for Indian Skin?

Korean skincare brands are designed to be gentle yet potent, making them well-suited for Indian skin, which often faces challenges like pigmentation, oiliness, and pollution. These products focus on hydration, barrier repair, and sun protection—key needs for Indian consumers. Many brands use natural extracts such as green tea, rice, and snail mucin, which help address common concerns without harsh chemicals.

### Why Use a Food Delivery Coupon Code?

A food coupon code is more than just a discount—it’s a smart way to order more for less. Whether you’re grabbing your morning coffee, ordering lunch during a busy workday, or planning a movie-night dinner, these coupons offer deals like flat ₹100 off, 50% discounts, buy-one-get-one-free offers, and even free delivery.

Here are some of the best reasons to use food delivery coupons:

- Save Money Instantly: No minimum order value? Great. Just apply the code and enjoy instant savings.

- Wide Availability: These codes are often valid at top restaurants near you, making it easy to find food offers today near me.

- Repeat Savings: Some apps like Swiggy and Zomato offer daily deals, allowing you to save every time you order.

## 1\. Smartphone Blowout: Up to 40% Off Latest Models

This month, several top retailers are offering huge discounts on smartphones, including popular brands like Samsung, Google Pixel, and even select iPhones. Whether you need an upgrade or you’re gifting one to someone special, now’s a great time to score a premium phone at a fraction of the price.

Top Picks:

- Samsung Galaxy S23 Ultra – Save $300 with trade-in.

- Google Pixel 8 – Up to 25% off unlocked models.

- iPhone 14 – Available with carrier deals or up to $200 off with select retailers.


> Deal Tip: Check bundle offers that include accessories like cases or earbuds for added value.

## 2\. Amazon Electronics Sale: Up to 60% Off

Amazon is running one of its biggest electronics sales this month, and the savings are steep. From smart home devices to headphones and streaming sticks, it’s a great time to revamp your tech setup.

What’s Hot:

- Echo Dot (5th Gen) – $22.99 (originally $49.99)

- Fire TV Stick 4K Max – $29.99 (was $54.99)

- JBL Bluetooth Speakers – up to 50% off


> Pro Tip: Use Amazon coupons and Lightning Deals for extra savings.

## 3\. Fashion Flash Sales: Up to 70% Off on Apparel and Footwear

Spring wardrobe refresh? You’re in luck. Major clothing retailers and online boutiques are hosting flash sales and clearance events on seasonal collections. Look out for offers from Zara, H&M, ASOS, and Nordstrom Rack.

Highlights:

- Women’s dresses from $12.99

- Men’s activewear up to 60% off

- Branded sneakers: Nike, Adidas, Puma – from $39.99


> Style Hack: Combine promo codes with sale items for double the discount.

## 4\. Beauty Bundles & Skincare Sets: Save Up to 50%

Beauty retailers like Sephora, Ulta, and Cult Beauty are offering deep discounts on value sets and brand bundles. If you’ve been eyeing high-end skincare or makeup products, this is your chance to grab them for less.

Top Beauty Deals:

- The Ordinary skincare bundle – $25 (valued at $48)

- Fenty Beauty value sets – up to 30% off

- Tarte Cosmetics friends & family sale – 40% sitewide


> Insider Tip: Look for free deluxe samples and gift-with-purchase offers to boost your haul.

## 5\. Home Essentials Clearance: Up to 65% Off

Looking to upgrade your home for less? Retailers like Wayfair, Target, and Home Depot are running monthly clearance sales on furniture, kitchenware, and decor.

Top Home Finds:

- Memory foam mattresses starting at $199

- Cookware sets up to 55% off

- Decorative lighting and rugs – limited-time deals from $39.99


> Smart Shopper Tip: Stack clearance deals with cashback apps or loyalty rewards.

## 6\. Streaming Service Discounts: Save on Monthly Subscriptions

Several streaming platforms are running promotional pricing for new and returning users. Whether you’re into movies, sports, or documentaries, you can enjoy top-tier content for less.

Best Offers This Month:

- Hulu – $1.99/month for 3 months (new users)

- HBO Max – 40% off annual plan

- Disney+ bundle with Hulu and ESPN+ – discounted for the first year


> Deal Alert: Cancel and resubscribe under a new promo if eligible — just check the fine print!

## 7\. Travel Deals: Flights & Hotels Up to 50% Off

With summer on the horizon, it’s the perfect time to book a getaway at discounted rates. Airlines and booking platforms like Expedia, Booking.com, and Skyscanner are running flash sales and last-minute travel deals.

Travel Bargains to Watch:

- Domestic flights from $49 (Southwest, JetBlue)

- Up to 40% off international hotel stays

- Travel bundle deals (flight + hotel) for extra savings


> Travel Hack: Use fare alert tools and loyalty points for maximum savings.

## 8\. Fitness Equipment & Gear: Up to 60% Off

If you’re building a home gym or upgrading your workout gear, this month brings some unbeatable prices on everything from dumbbells to fitness trackers.

Hot Fitness Deals:

- Adjustable dumbbells – 50% off

- Fitbit & Garmin trackers – starting at $79

- Yoga mats, resistance bands, and apparel – major markdowns


> Bonus Tip: Check for free shipping thresholds and first-time customer discounts.

## 9\. Grocery & Meal Kit Discounts: Save Big on Food Bills

Looking to cut costs on your weekly grocery haul? Online grocery stores and meal delivery services are offering first-time user discounts and loyalty deals.

Tasty Savings:

- HelloFresh – 60% off your first box + free shipping

- Instacart – up to $20 off your first order

- Walmart+ grocery deals with exclusive digital coupons


> Pro Tip: Sign up for newsletters or referral links to unlock deeper discounts.

## 10\. Online Course & eLearning Deals: Learn for Less

It’s never a bad time to invest in yourself, especially when online learning platforms are offering steep course discounts. Whether it’s career development or personal growth, you can find courses starting under $20.

Great eLearning Offers:

- Udemy: Courses from $12.99 (limited time)

- Coursera: Free trial + up to 40% off certificate programs

- Skillshare: 1 free month + exclusive member discounts


> Level Up Tip: Look for bundle pricing or limited-time masterclass access.

## Final Thoughts: Make the Most of These Monthly Deals

The best deals of the month span nearly every category — from tech to travel, fashion to fitness. But remember, many of these discounts are limited-time or while supplies last, so acting quickly ensures you won’t miss out.

Here’s a quick way to stay ahead:

- Bookmark deal sites or install browser extensions that track promo codes.

- Sign up for retailer newsletters for early access or exclusive offers.

- Use cashback platforms to stack savings on top of discounts.


Whether you’re treating yourself, upgrading your lifestyle, or shopping smart for the season, these top 10 deals offer unbeatable value.

# Best Gaming Consoles Recommendations

By [Nik](https://neudeals.co/blog/author/nik/ "Posts by Nik")January 6, 2025June 17th, 2025[No Comments](https://neudeals.co/blog/best-games-consoles/#respond)11 min read

## Best gaming consoles in 2025: every major system tested and ranked

Finding the best gaming console is incredibly important, whether you’re an experienced player or looking to get into gaming in 2025. There are loads of options out there right now, all designed to cater to a variety of different requirements and playstyles, which can make it a little tricky to find the right one for you if you’re not already wedded to one particular ecosystem.

Table of Contents

[Toggle](https://neudeals.co/blog/best-games-consoles/#)

That’s exactly where I come in, as I’ve spent hundreds of hours playing with the offerings from each major console brand to deliver expert buying advice. We’ve also been testing consoles for almost two decades here at Neu, with detailed reviews going back to the Wii. When weighing up which ones to include in this list, I considered everything from my own experience to the individual specs, performance, design, and, perhaps most importantly of all, price and value for money.

I know first-hand that every option included here will deliver a fantastic experience in its specific niche, so you can shop for any of them with confidence.

## Best gaming console in 2025

As someone with 25 years of experience writing about video games and editing Official PlayStation Magazine and several Xbox magazines, I’m here to share why each of the top game consoles deserves your attention. With so many great options available right now, each console on my list offers unique advantages and features—something I can personally attest to, as I own most of them.

## _1\. PlayStation 5_

![PlayStation 5](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20600%20309'%2F%3E)

### Best games console overall

₹44,990 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Supports 4K and 120fps
- Brilliant controller
- Speedy and slick UI
- Fast SSD storage

#### **Cons:**

- Internal storage fills up quick
- Large overall size

The PS5 Slim is now the dominant PS5 model, replacing the chunkier launch version on store shelves. It’s considerably smaller, but still boasts the same incredible gaming experiences and even benefits from some much-needed quality of life upgrades.

With a library of incredible exclusives, including Marvel’s Spider-Man 2, Stellar Blade, and The Last of Us Part 1, the PS5 Slim is absolutely one of the best consoles that you can buy right now. It might not be quite as powerful as the Xbox Series X on paper, but I’ve found that it still offers superb performance in a wide range of first and third-party titles. You can even expect to experience certain games with ray tracing and at resolutions up to 4K.

The included controller, Sony’s DualSense Wireless Controller, is also a major highlight in my eyes, thanks to its seriously comfortable form factor and fantastic haptic feedback. Unfortunately, my tests show that features do drain its battery life fast, so expect to spend a fair amount of time topping it up between play sessions.

CPU: AMD Zen 2-based CPU with 8 cores at 3.5GHz

GPU: 10.28 TFLOPs, 36 CUs at 2.23GHz

Memory: 16GB

Internal storage: 825GB / 1TB

Expandable storage: NVMe SSD

Optical Drive: 4K UHD Blu-ray Drive

## _2\. Xbox Series X_

![Xbox Series X](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20600%20309'%2F%3E)

### Best budget gaming consoles

₹34,990 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- A fast and powerful games console
- Game Pass is incredible value
- Play in 4K at 120fps
- Great backwards compatibility

#### **Cons:**

- Lacks compelling exclusives
- UI is workable but clumsy
- Clunky UI compared to PS5

From a raw technical perspective, the Xbox Series X is comfortably the most powerful console currently available in the Xbox ecosystem. I can say that a lot of the best Xbox Series X games look and sound phenomenal, thanks in no small part to the stellar implementation of Dolby Atmos surround sound and Dolby Vision, which complement the console’s high-end specs very well.

Priced at $499 / £479, the Xbox Series X is far better than what you would be getting for that price in the gaming PC or gaming laptop market. Like the PS5, the Xbox Series X also has 4K support and compatibility with desirable graphical features like ray tracing. As the system utilizes Gen 4.0 NVMe SSD tech for its storage, it is also unlikely to struggle when it comes to storage speeds any time soon.

The one area where the Xbox Series X may disappoint is in its software library, which lacks the many compelling console exclusives that you can find on PS5. Xbox Game Pass, however, does help make up for this, offering players the chance to tap into a wide range of titles for a low monthly price.

There is also excellent backwards compatibility, far better than what the PS5 offers, which allows you to play games dating back to the original Xbox. I’ve played a huge variety of older games this way, and it’s become one of the main reasons that I turn on my Xbox Series X.

CPU: 8x Cores @ 3.8 GHz (3.6 GHz w/ SMT) Custom Zen 2 CPU

GPU: 12 TFLOPS, 52 CUs @ 1.825 GHz Custom RDNA 2 GPU

Memory: 16 GB

Internal Storage: 1TB

Expandable Storage: 1TB Expansion Card

Optical Drive: 4K UHD Blu-ray Drive

## _3\. Nintendo Switch OLED_

![Nintendo Switch OLED](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20676%20360'%2F%3E)

### Most powerful gaming consoles

₹28,120 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Great OLED display
- Loads of fantastic games

- Quality exclusive games

#### **Cons:**

- TV mode lacks upgrades
- Lacking performance
- Base model can be cheaper

The Nintendo Switch OLED is easily the definitive model out of Nintendo’s current consoles. Its internal processing power might be the same as the standard Nintendo Switch model, but its screen has been upgraded significantly. With a bigger 7-inch OLED panel, the best Nintendo Switch games look better than ever before when playing in handheld mode.

At $349.99 / £299.99, the Nintendo Switch OLED is more expensive than the standard Switch model, but is still much more affordable than Sony and Microsoft’s current-gen consoles, the PS5 and Xbox Series X. Of course, it’s worth noting that the Nintendo Switch OLED isn’t as powerful as these rival consoles, and those who play games ported to the system from other platforms may see them struggle slightly at times in comparison.
