## _4\. Valve Steam Deck_

![Valve Steam Deck](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20522%20294'%2F%3E)

### Best PC games console

₹64,990 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Excellent performance
- Huge library of PC and indie games
- Innovative mix of power and design

#### **Cons:**

- Innovative mix of power and design

Valve’s Steam Deck is the Holy Grail of handheld consoles – it enables you to play the latest PC games on the go. It’s larger and heavier than a Nintendo Switch but crams in all sorts of high-spec features, such as a top-end custom AMD GPU and CPU for power, touch pads, gyro controls and more.

The idea is you can take your PC collection on the go, and with a classy 800p display (there is a new OLED model too) and supporting 30 and 60 frames per second the Valve Steam Deck performs well, though dedicated PC gamers may not like the downgrade. Yet, the handheld console supports over 3,000 games and more can be made to work with some effort.

And here’s the rub, Steam Deck is impressive and can run the latest and best PC games, including 2022’s biggest hits like [Elden Ring](https://www.creativebloq.com/reviews/elden-ring), yet games aren’t made for Steam Deck, they simply run _on_ Steam Deck. It’s a subtle difference, as games made for Nintendo Switch are designed for its controls, while on Steam Deck you’ll need to spend time figuring out control setups that work. Similarly, battery life can drain fast (two hours for a Triple-A game) unless you monitor and tweak each game’s settings – Steam Deck is hardly plug-and-play.

Steam Deck is also heavy and noisy, the fan really works overtime to cool this beast. Yet, it’s a gaming PC in your hands and PC gamers will be used to the DIY nature of trouble-shooting game setups and controls.

Dimensions: ‎16 x 5 x 3 inches

GPU: AMD RDNA 2

RAM: 16GB

Max Resolution: 800p

Optical Drive: SSD

Storage: 64 – 512GB

## _5\. Nintendo Switch Lite_

![Evercade EXP](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20600%20309'%2F%3E)

### Best gaming consoles for kids

₹16,999 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Light and small
- Very durable
- Comfortable to use
- Great color options
- Affordable price for a console

#### **Cons:**

- No built-in rumble or kickstand
- Limited to handheld games

The Nintendo Switch Lite is our number-one choice for those who want a console for kids and families. Not only does it have a lower asking price than the standard Nintendo Switch and Nintendo Switch OLED models at $199.99 / £200, but its smaller size is ideal for little hands (or just those who prefer a more compact system). Beyond that, there are plenty of bright, cheery colors to choose from, such as blue, pink, purple, and yellow.

With a thick plastic construction, I found that the Nintendo Switch Lite is more durable than the standard Nintendo Switch model, which means that it should hold up a little better in the long-term. Despite owning other models, I always rely on the Nintendo Switch Lite while I’m travelling, as I don’t think it’s likely to pick up any major damage thanks to this.

Unlike the regular Switch, the Joy-Con controllers can’t be removed either, which means that they can’t get lost.

The screen is on the smaller side, especially when compared to the Nintendo Switch OLED model, but it still runs at the same 720p resolution as the Nintendo Switch and OLED. Its performance is the same, too, so you don’t need to worry about games running slower just because you’re using a smaller system.

However, it’s worth keeping in mind the Nintendo Switch Lite’s major limitation – it can’t be docked and connected to a TV in order to play in TV mode. Instead, it’s a solely portable system.

## _6\. Evercade EXP_

![Evercade EXP](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20600%20309'%2F%3E)

### The best retro games console

₹6,893 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Fantastic screen
- Excellent built-in games library
- TATE mode is a good option

#### **Cons:**

- TATE mode feels cramped
- The cartridge slot is tight
- New EXP-R model is better

The Evercade EXP stands out as the best retro gaming console available today. Unlike mini replicas like the Atari 400 Mini or consoles designed solely for older games, such as the Atari 2600+, the Evercade EXP offers a unique blend. It plays collections of classic games from the 8, 16, and 32-bit eras, alongside new indie titles and modern retro-inspired games.

With a vast library of game collections, often affordably priced with six to eight games each, the Evercade EXP is an excellent entry point for retro gaming enthusiasts. The console’s hardware shines as well, with an OLED screen that brings vibrant life to retro games and a comfortable, responsive handheld design. Plus, it can connect to a TV, expanding its versatility. The TATE mode is a notable feature, allowing vertical gameplay for classic shooters, though it can feel slightly gimmicky due to the close placement of buttons, as mentioned in my Evercade EXP review.

Overall, the Evercade EXP offers incredible value for money. It comes pre-installed with 18 Capcom classics, including Final Fight, Ghouls ‘n Ghosts, and Strider. A special bundle includes the IREM Arcade 1 collection, featuring iconic ’80s arcade titles like R-Type.

Additionally, Evercade has released the new EXP-R model, featuring a sleek black design with teal accents. This updated version boasts a cooler-running chipset and a textured grip for added comfort. It supports the entire Evercade cartridge library and includes Tomb Raider in the bundle, enhancing its appeal for retro gaming fans.

Dimensions: 7.76 x 3.1 x 0.8 inches

Screen: 4.3 inch IPS Screen (800 x480)

Storage: 4GB

Portable battery life: approx 5 hours

#### Why We Picked It

The Meta Quest 3 costs more than the Quest 3S, but its higher-resolution display and wider field of view make it worth the extra investment.

₹54,170 at Amazon₹59,990 at Flipkart

#### Pros:

- Color Pass-Through Cameras
- Enjoy sharp, detailed visuals for an immersive experience.
- Seamless performance for all your tasks.
- Built for maximum comfort during extended use.

#### Cons:

- Limited battery lifespan
- Missing eye-tracking technology

Beyond these upgrades, it’s almost identical to Meta’s newer headset, featuring a color passthrough camera for mixed reality and the robust Qualcomm Snapdragon XR2 Gen 2 processor. Interestingly, these features surpass those of the more expensive, eye-tracking Meta Quest Pro in certain aspects.

Looking for a VR experience without the hassle of cables? The Quest 3 is your perfect standalone headset. With sharper visuals and a wider field of view, it delivers an immersive experience that stands out. Considering its price and features, the Quest 3 is the top choice for diving into VR without needing a tethered setup or an expensive PC (or the Apple Vision Pro).

## Best AR/VR Interface

### Apple Vision Pro

![Apple Vision Pro](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20601%20338'%2F%3E)

#### Why We Picked It

Apple launched the Vision Pro on February 2, 2024, a groundbreaking AR/VR headset that operates entirely through hand gestures, eye movements, and Siri voice commands—no controllers needed. With 4K graphics, spatial audio, and advanced sensor tracking, it offers a truly immersive experience.

$3499.00 at Apple.com

#### Pros:

- Exceptional AR/VR interface design
- Industry-leading eye and hand tracking technology
- Controller-free operation for seamless interaction
- Vibrant, high-resolution display
- Superior video passthrough quality
- Extensive VisionOS apps and innovative features

#### Cons:

- High price point
- Limited battery life
- Unbalanced, front-heavy design can cause discomfort
- Inconsistent compatibility with some iPad apps

The Vision Pro marks Apple’s debut in AR and VR, though the company prefers to refer to it as a “spatial computer” rather than a headset. Regardless of the label (we consider it an AR/VR/mixed reality headset), the Vision Pro is an ambitious device designed for both work and play. It uses eye and hand tracking to provide intuitive, natural controls that surpass any system we’ve tested. As a first-generation product on the newly launched VisionOS, it’s surprisingly feature-rich, offering some of the best hardware ever incorporated into a consumer-grade head-mounted display.

### What Are the Best VR Headsets in 2025?

Virtual reality has evolved rapidly, and 2025 offers a diverse lineup of VR headsets tailored for gaming, productivity, fitness, and immersive experiences. But what exactly makes a VR headset the “best”? The answer depends on your needs—whether you want the most advanced features, the best value, or a device specialized for gaming, work, or mixed reality.

### How to Choose the Best VR Headset

Selecting the best VR headset starts with understanding your priorities:

- **Usage Needs:** Are you focused on gaming, professional applications, fitness, or a mix of everything? For gaming, PSVR 2 and Meta Quest 3 are top picks. For productivity and spatial computing, Apple Vision Pro and HTC Vive Focus Vision stand out.
- **Type of Device:** Decide between standalone (wireless, all-in-one) headsets like Meta Quest 3, or PC-tethered models like HTC Vive Pro 2 for higher performance.
- **Budget:** Entry-level options like Meta Quest 3S offer great value, while premium devices like Apple Vision Pro deliver cutting-edge features at a higher price.
- **Comfort and Features:** Consider weight, adjustability, display quality, tracking, and controller haptics for the most comfortable and immersive experience.

## 1.DJIMini4Pro

![DJI Mini 4 Pro](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%201985%20946'%2F%3E)

### Best Drone for Instagram Reels and TikTok Videos

₹9,800 Buy At Amazon₹894 Buy At Flipkart

#### **Pros**:

- The DJI Mini 4 Pro offers impressive 4K video at 60fps and 48MP stills, delivering excellent image quality.
- Enhanced obstacle avoidance sensors provide safer, more reliable flight in complex environments.

#### **Cons:**

- Its lightweight design makes it less stable in strong winds, impacting performance in harsh weather.
- The camera lacks full 360° rotation, limiting shooting flexibility for certain angles.

While the DJI Air 2s and Mavic 3 deliver exceptional aerial image quality, they fall short when it comes to capturing footage in portrait orientation. For those planning to create content for platforms like TikTok or Instagram Reels, this means you’ll have to crop your video, sacrificing resolution and making composition more challenging during shoots.

The Mini 3 Pro eliminates this issue. With a simple tap, its camera flips into portrait mode, allowing you to capture content in full 4K resolution using the entire sensor. It supports video at up to 60 frames per second and takes stunning 48-megapixel stills in DNG format.

Its compact, foldable design makes it slightly larger than a standard can of Coke, yet it’s packed with sensors to help prevent collisions with obstacles. However, due to its small size and lightweight (249g), it can struggle in strong winds, which may reduce flight times in blustery conditions.

## 2.DJIAir3

![DJI Air 3](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%201419%20711'%2F%3E)

### The best drone for aerial photos and videos

₹1,499 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Dual-camera system with wide and telephoto lenses provides versatile shooting options for creative content.
- Extended flight time of up to 46 minutes allows for longer aerial sessions without frequent recharging.

#### **Cons**:

- Heavier build compared to compact models may reduce portability for casual users.
- Higher price point makes it less accessible for beginners or budget-conscious buyers.

The DJI Air 3 is beginner-friendly, boasts an impressive 46-minute battery life, and features a dual-camera system, offering greater versatility and more creative shot options compared to its predecessor. Opting for the DJI Air 3 Fly More Combo is highly recommended, as it includes the DJI RC 2 controller with a built-in screen—a significant upgrade over the standard controller that relies on your phone for live view.

With omnidirectional obstacle sensing, including side detection, the Air 3 provides enhanced safety and usability. Its improved ActiveTrack feature allows the drone to autonomously follow and film a subject while skillfully avoiding obstacles, making it ideal for dynamic shooting scenarios.

The drone maintains steady positioning even in moderate winds, ensuring smooth cinematography. Compact and portable, the Air 3 measures 8 x 3.5 x 3.25 inches when folded and weighs about 1.5 pounds, fitting comfortably into most standard backpacks for easy transport.

## 3.DJIAvata2

![DJI Avata 2](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20623%20354'%2F%3E)

### Best FPV drone for first-person flying

₹5,877 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Compact and durable design paired with 4K video at 100fps offers thrilling, high-quality first-person footage.
- Speeds of up to 27 m/s and intuitive controls make it ideal for dynamic and agile flight experiences.

#### **Cons**:

- Limited 20-minute battery life may require frequent recharging for extended sessions.
- Advanced manual mode requires an additional FPV Remote Controller 3, adding to the cost.

All our drone picks can capture stunning, high-resolution aerial footage, but the DJI Avata 2 stands out by prioritizing speed and agility over smooth cinematic shots, delivering an exhilarating, onboard experience with every flight.

This compact, dynamic drone—DJI’s third-generation first-person-view (FPV) model—makes the FPV format more accessible and practical than ever. It features a 1/1.3-inch sensor capable of shooting 4K video at up to 100 fps, powerful motors that reach speeds of 27 meters per second (compared to 21 meters per second for the Air 3 and Mavic 3 Pro), and a durable plastic shell designed to withstand impacts, whether it’s a tree or a wall.

The recommended Fly More Combo includes an updated headset, three batteries offering around 20 minutes of flight time each, and a compact version of the motion controller first introduced with the original DJI FPV drone. This controller simplifies flying for beginners, but for those seeking to master the advanced manual mode, DJI’s FPV Remote Controller 3 is a must-have accessory.

## 4.DJIMini3Pro

![Best Drone](data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D'http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg'%20viewBox%3D'0%200%20521%20288'%2F%3E)

### The Best Drone DJI Mini 3 Pro for Videos

₹2,996 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Camera rotates to shoot true vertical video for TikTok or Instagram, compact size
- Compact, lightweight design with portrait shooting mode and 4K/60fps capability, perfect for social media content.

#### **Cons**:

- Small size is more susceptible to strong winds
- Lightweight build struggles in strong winds, reducing stability and flight time.

While DJI’s Air 2s and Mavic 3 deliver stunning aerial image quality, they fall short for social media creators by lacking a portrait shooting mode. To adapt footage for platforms like TikTok or Instagram Reels, you’d need to crop the video down the middle, sacrificing resolution and making on-location composition more challenging.

The Mini 3 Pro solves this problem effortlessly. With a simple tap on the screen, its camera flips into portrait orientation, enabling you to capture content optimized for social media at the sensor’s full 4K resolution. You can record videos at up to 60 frames per second and capture high-quality stills in DNG format with a remarkable 48-megapixel resolution.

Designed with portability in mind, the Mini 3 Pro folds down to a compact size—just slightly larger than a standard soda can. Despite its small stature, it’s equipped with advanced sensors to help avoid obstacles like trees. However, its lightweight 249g design makes it more susceptible to strong winds, which can reduce flight times in blustery conditions.

For creators prioritizing versatility, portability, and high-quality social content, the Mini 3 Pro is an ideal choice.

The IZI Spectra Drone is a high-performance aerial device designed for enthusiasts and professionals seeking advanced imaging capabilities. Equipped with a 1-inch CMOS sensor, it captures 4K HDR videos and 20MP photos, ensuring exceptional image quality across various lighting conditions.

- **Advanced Imaging:** The 1-inch CMOS sensor delivers high-resolution visuals, complemented by a 6x digital zoom and a 90° field of view, allowing for versatile shooting angles.
- **Extended Flight Time:** With dual 5000mAh batteries, the IZI Spectra offers up to 102 minutes of flight time, enabling longer aerial sessions.
- **Obstacle Avoidance:** The Tri-Detect Obstacle Sensing system provides front, rear, and downward sensors, enhancing flight safety by detecting and avoiding obstacles.
- **Night Vision Mode:** This feature allows for clear imaging in low-light environments, expanding creative possibilities during dawn, dusk, or nighttime operations.
- **Long-Range Connectivity:** The drone supports a flight range of up to 22 kilometers, with stable 100Mbps video transmission, facilitating extensive coverage for various projects.
- **Multiple Flight Modes:** Includes Follow Me, Active Track, Orbit Mode, Hyperlapse, and Waypoint Mission Planning, offering diverse options for dynamic and creative aerial shots.
- **Storage Options:** Comes with 8GB of built-in storage, expandable up to 128GB via a Class 10, UHS-I SD card, accommodating extensive footage needs.

### The Best drone for serious photographers

₹21,657 Buy At Amazon

#### **Pros**:

- Superb image quality, superb flight times, relatively compact size
- Three useful cameras
- Solid safety features

#### **Cons**:

- Higher price puts it out of reach for many
- C2 European rating
- Not everyone will need three cameras

The Mavic 3 Pro earned Awards 2023 for Best Camera Product, and for good reason. It’s the first consumer drone with three cameras, making it a top choice for aerial photographers and filmmakers seeking unmatched versatility. Its primary 20MP Four Thirds sensor, shared with the Mavic 3 Classic, delivers exceptional image quality with an adjustable aperture and pro-level video capabilities. Additionally, its 3x and 7x telephoto cameras provide impressive image quality for those needing extra reach beyond the main 24mm focal length.

Despite its professional-grade features, the Mavic 3 Pro remains easy to fly, even in windy conditions, thanks to intuitive controls and robust safety features. However, its placement in the restrictive C2 European drone category due to its weight and its premium price make it less ideal for beginners. That said, it’s a more affordable and portable alternative to the Inspire 3, offering unmatched versatility for professionals.

### What Makes the Best Drone in 2025?

When searching for the best drone in 2025, it’s essential to consider what features truly matter for your needs. Are you a photographer, content creator, or just a hobbyist looking for top performance? The best drones today combine advanced camera systems, intelligent flight features, and user-friendly controls to deliver stunning aerial footage and reliable performance.

### How to Choose the Best Drone for You

Selecting the best drone depends on your priorities:

- **Camera Quality:** Look for drones with large sensors, high megapixel counts, and advanced video capabilities. The DJI Air 3S, for example, features a dual camera system (50MP wide-angle and 48MP telephoto) and supports 4K/120fps video for cinematic results.
- **Flight Time:** Longer battery life means more time in the air. Top models like the Air 3S offer up to 45 minutes per charge.
- **Portability:** Compact drones such as the DJI Mini 4 Pro are lightweight, foldable, and easy to carry—ideal for travelers and beginners.
- **Obstacle Avoidance:** Advanced obstacle detection and avoidance systems make flying safer and more accessible for all skill levels.
- **Ease of Use:** Intuitive controls, smart tracking, and automated flight modes help beginners capture professional-looking footage without a steep learning curve.
- **Budget:** There are excellent drones available at various price points, from entry-level options like the DJI Neo to mid-range and professional models.

## Myntra Offers and Deals

Myntra is your ultimate destination for fashion and lifestyle apparel, offering a seamless shopping experience with a wide range of products across various categories for customers throughout India. While the branded items on Myntra can be a bit pricey, you can grab them at discounted rates by taking advantage of the latest Myntra Coupons. These promotional deals allow you to enjoy stylish and high-quality items without breaking the bank.

## Myntra FAQs

#### **1\. How can I find the latest Myntra offers and discounts?**

Myntra frequently updates its offers on the official website and app. You can find the latest Myntra discount codes, festive sales, and exclusive bank offers on the “Offers” section of the platform. Additionally, subscribing to Myntra’s newsletter or following them on social media ensures you never miss out on deals.

#### 2\. Does Myntra provide first-time user discounts?

Yes, Myntra offers special discounts for first-time users, usually in the form of flat percentage discounts or cashback offers. To avail of these, create a new account and apply the provided Myntra first-order coupon at checkout. The discount may vary based on ongoing promotions.

#### 3\. What is Myntra Insider, and how does it help in getting extra discounts?

Myntra Insider is a loyalty program that rewards frequent shoppers with exclusive discounts, early access to sales, free shipping, and extra cashback offers. Members earn Insider Points on every purchase, which can be redeemed for additional discounts or partnered brand deals.

#### 4\. Are there any Myntra bank or wallet offers available?

Yes, Myntra regularly collaborates with banks and digital wallets like HDFC, ICICI, Paytm, and PhonePe to provide additional discounts, cashback, or EMI options. These offers usually require using a specific card or payment method at checkout and are updated frequently.

#### 5\. When is the next Myntra sale, and what discounts can I expect?

Myntra hosts multiple sales throughout the year, such as the End of Reason Sale (EORS), Big Fashion Festival, and Festive Season Sale. Discounts range from 40% to 80% off on top fashion brands, along with extra cashback and bank offers. Keep an eye on Myntra’s website and app for announcements about the next big sale.

## How to apply the Myntra Coupon Code?

- **Add Items to Cart**: Select all the products you want and add them to your cart.
- **Go to the Cart**: Once you’re ready to checkout, visit your cart. You’ll find the “Apply Coupon” section in the top right corner.
- **Log In**: If you’re not already logged in, do so to access your account. Then, choose the coupon that offers the highest discount from the list.
- **Apply the Coupon**: Once the coupon is successfully applied, the total amount will reflect the discount.
- **Using Neu Deals Codes**: If you’ve selected a coupon from Neu Deals, a pop-up window will show the code. Simply copy it by clicking the “Copy Code” button, and paste it into the coupon section at checkout to get the discount.

### FAQs

#### Who are Myntra’s delivery partners?

Myntra collaborates with several prominent logistics companies to ensure fast and efficient delivery, including Blue Dart Express, DHL, DTDC, Ecom Express, and others. The platform works hard to deliver orders as quickly as possible, providing a seamless shopping experience for customers.

#### How does Myntra handle refunds?

If you paid for your order using a prepaid method, the refund will be processed to the same payment method. For Cash on Delivery (COD) orders, you can provide your bank details for a refund or opt for Myntra credit, depending on your preference.

## About Domino’s

Domino’s was founded in 1960 by Tom Monaghan in Michigan, USA. Today, it has grown into one of the largest pizza chains in the world, with over 20,900 stores across more than 90 countries. It is renowned for its extensive variety of pizzas, which can be customized with a range of toppings, crusts, and sauces.

They also offer a variety of other items, including pasta, chicken wings, garlic bread, desserts, and more. One of its popular features is the “Tracker,” which lets you follow your pizza from when it’s being made to when it’s out for delivery. With its simple and fast ordering system and great service, Domino’s has become a favorite pizza choice for millions of people worldwide.

## How Do I Get Promo Codes for Domino’s?

If you’re looking for an active coupon code for Domino’s, check out GrabOn. We regularly update our coupons to bring you the best deals available. Following their social media channels like [Instagram](https://www.instagram.com/dominos/?hl=en), [Facebook](https://www.facebook.com/dominos), [TikTok](https://www.tiktok.com/@dominos?lang=en), and [X](https://twitter.com/dominos?ref_src=twsrc%5Egoogle%7Ctwcamp%5Eserp%7Ctwgr%5Eauthor) is another great way to stay updated on upcoming sales. Moreover, keep an eye on the official website so you don’t miss out on any flash or seasonal sales.

2. **Add Items to Cart:** Choose your favorite pizzas—including the Big Big 6-in-1 Pizza, Volcano Pizza, or Cheese Volcano Pizza.

4. **Enjoy Savings on Your Meal:** Most codes can be applied to a wide range of menu items, and some are exclusive to first-time or app users.

### More Ways to Save at Domino’s

## Frequently Asked Questions

### What are Domino’s coupon codes?

Domino’s coupon codes are special alphanumeric codes that unlock discounts, cashback, or other offers when ordering online or via the Domino’s app. You can use these codes to save money on pizzas, sides, desserts, and more.

### Where can I find the latest Domino’s coupon codes?

You can find updated Domino’s coupon codes on their official website, on the deals or great offers page in the Domino’s app, and on reputable coupon platforms like CouponDunia, GrabOn, 99CashDeals, and other coupon websites.

### How do I use a Domino’s coupon code?

To use a coupon code:

1. Add your favorite items to the Domino’s cart online or in the app.
2. Go to the checkout page.
3. Look for the section labeled “Apply Coupon,” “Promo Code,” or similar.
4. Enter (or paste) the coupon code and click “Apply.”
5. The discount should appear before you finalize payment.

### Can I use more than one coupon code in a single order?

No, typically only one Domino’s coupon code or offer can be applied per order. You’ll need to choose the code that provides the best value for your purchase.

### Are there special Domino’s coupons for new users?

Yes, Domino’s frequently offers exclusive discounts for first-time users, such as a flat ₹100 off or other attractive deals on the first order placed through the app or website.

### Do Domino’s coupon codes work for both delivery and takeaway?

Most Domino’s coupon codes are valid for delivery, takeaway, and dine-in orders unless specified otherwise. Always check the coupon terms for restrictions.

### Why isn’t my Domino’s coupon code working?

Some reasons could be:

1. The coupon code has expired.
2. The offer is only valid for specific menu items or order values.
3. The code is valid for first-time users only.
4. You may have already used the code or are not meeting the minimum purchase requirement.

### Does Domino’s offer cashback or wallet deals?

Yes, Domino’s sometimes partners with digital wallets like Paytm or banks to provide cashback or promotional deals in addition to regular coupon codes.

### Are there Domino’s coupon codes for bulk or party orders?

Yes, there are specific coupon codes for large orders, combo deals, and parties. Check for banners or coupon platforms listing discounts on combos or orders above a certain value.

### How often are Domino’s coupon codes updated?

Domino’s and major coupon websites update their coupon codes regularly, sometimes daily or for limited-time campaigns around festivals, weekends, and special events. It’s best to check current codes each time you order.

## Why Shop with H&M?

## Key Tips for H&M Shoppers

### H&M Savings Tips

- **Check Promotional Offers:** Visit the main page for your location to view current promotions. These can include free shipping on all orders, percentage discounts, or flat-rate discounts.
- **Explore the Sale Page:** Navigate to the Sale section via the top menu to discover products available at discounted prices.
- **Join the H&M Loyalty Club:** Sign up to receive 10% off your next order and earn points for every dollar spent. Accumulate 200 points to get a $5 reward, and enjoy exclusive offers and discounts as a member.
- **Utilize Financing Options:** Members of the loyalty club can take advantage of “Pay Later by Klarna” to earn double points on their purchases.
- **Student Discount:** Students can save 10% on their orders. Click on the “Student Discount” link at the top left of the main page to sign up through Student Beans and verify your student status.
- **Find Current Offers:** Browse the shopping navigation menu and hover over Women, Divided, Men, Kids, or H&M Home. Look for the “Offers & Deals” section to view the latest offers in each category.
- **Free Shipping:** Enjoy free shipping on orders over $40 before taxes. For orders under $40 or if you need expedited shipping, a fee will apply.

Men can explore a variety of items, including trousers, hoodies, jackets, shirts, t-shirts, jeans, and underwear at the H&M online store. Additionally, you can opt for concept collections such as Basics, Divided, and Trend for curated fashion choices.

### Kids

H&M offers stylish options for kids, perfect for any occasion. From festive outfits like Diwali dresses to Halloween costumes, you’ll find a range of choices for children. The store also features designer apparel for infants, ensuring even the littlest ones look their best.

## Types of Redbus Coupons

Redbus offers a variety of coupons to suit different travel needs. Here are some of the most popular types of Redbus Coupons:

### 1\. **Flat Discount Coupons**

These coupons offer a flat discount on your total booking amount. For example, you might get a 10% discount on your entire fare. Flat discount coupons are great for instant savings on any booking.

### 2\. **Cashback Coupons**

With cashback coupons, you can earn a certain percentage of your booking amount back as cashback. This cashback can be used for future bookings, adding even more value to your travel expenses.

### 3\. **Operator-Specific Coupons**

Some Redbus Coupons are exclusive to certain bus operators. These coupons provide special discounts when you book tickets with specific bus services. It’s a fantastic way to save if you have a preferred bus operator.

### 4\. **First-Time User Coupons**

If you’re new to Redbus, you can benefit from first-time user coupons. These offers are designed to welcome new customers with attractive discounts on their initial bookings.

### 5\. **Seasonal and Festival Offers**

During festive seasons and special occasions, Redbus rolls out exclusive coupons to celebrate with its users. Keep an eye out for these limited-time offers to maximize your savings.

## About Cleartrip

Cleartrip is a leading platform for all your travel needs, offering a wide range of services to simplify your journey. From its beginnings as a travel agency in 2006, founded by Hrush Bhatt and Stuart Crighton, Cleartrip has expanded to provide business travel solutions, one-touch bookings, holiday packages, bus reservations, and more. Operating in Oman, Qatar, Kuwait, Bahrain, and Saudi Arabia, it has become a trusted name in travel. The Cleartrip mobile app is a must-have for exclusive deals and discounts on flights, buses, and hotel bookings. Visit the site today and explore Cleartrip coupon codes to save on your next trip!

FirstCry’s mission is to make it easy and convenient for mothers to find everything they need for their little ones, all while providing a seamless online shopping experience.

## About Snitch

Founded in 2019 by Siddharth R Dungarwal, Snitch began as a B2B men’s apparel brand before transitioning to a D2C model during the pandemic. ## About Web Hosting

Web hosting is a service that enables individuals and businesses to make their websites available on the Internet. Essentially, it’s like renting storage space on a server where your website’s files, data, and content are stored. When someone types your domain name into their browser, the hosting server retrieves and displays those files, allowing visitors to access your site.

Web hosting is a key component of establishing an online presence. To make it more affordable, many providers offer discount coupons. However, it’s essential to carefully evaluate hosting providers to ensure they offer reliable service and meet your specific requirements.

## Where Can I Get Web Hosting Offers and Promo Codes?

At Neu Deals, we recognize the importance of managing expenses and sticking to your budget. That’s why we’ve curated a comprehensive list of Web Hosting offers and deals all in one place. Explore a wide range of ongoing promotions and exclusive Web Hosting coupons tailored to meet your needs. Simply choose the one that fits your order and apply it during checkout to enjoy instant savings.

### Types of Web Hosting

- **Shared Hosting**: In this type of hosting, multiple websites share the resources of a single server. It’s a cost-effective solution for small websites with moderate traffic.
- **VPS Hosting**: Virtual Private Server (VPS) hosting divides a physical server into multiple virtual servers, providing more control and dedicated resources than shared hosting.
- **Dedicated Hosting**: With dedicated hosting, an entire server is allocated to one user, offering maximum control, customization, and resources.
- **Web Hosting in India**: Delivering reliable solutions to ensure cool websites remain accessible to the world!

Popular companies like **[GoDaddy](https://neudeals.co/godaddy-coupons/ "GoDaddy Coupons")**, [**BigRock**](https://neudeals.co/bigrock-coupons/ "BigRock Coupons"), HostingRaja, [**Bluehost**](https://neudeals.co/bluehost-hosting-deal/ "Bluehost Hosting"), [**Hostinger**](https://neudeals.co/hostinger-coupons/ "Hostinger Coupons"), SpeedHost, and others offer affordable plans. They provide trial versions, flexible monthly and yearly subscriptions, a range of packages, and various categories to suit different needs, all at budget-friendly prices.

## About Udemy

Udemy is a leading online education platform that offers lifetime access to courses for students worldwide. Once enrolled, students can access their courses at any time, from anywhere, even after completion. It’s like having a personal teacher available at your convenience to help you understand your concepts.

Udemy stands out as one of the top online education platforms, connecting students and instructors globally. It goes beyond academic and business skills, offering courses for personal development as well.

With a library of over 65,000 courses, Udemy covers a wide range of topics, from programming and web design to photography and academic test prep. It is renowned for its top online courses and highly regarded certificates, which are valued by top employers globally. Trusted by students around the world, Udemy provides expert instruction across various subjects.

Udemy’s sophisticated and interactive learning methodologies are personalized to meet the needs of job seekers. It offers 24/7 tutorials on core business operations and technical skills from native and non-native speakers. Popular courses are available in multiple languages, including German, Spanish, Portuguese, Brazilian, Japanese, and French.

## How to Download the Udemy App?

Follow these simple steps to download the Udemy app on your Android smartphone:

1. **Open the Google Play Store:** On your smartphone, open the Google Play Store app.
2. **Search for Udemy:** In the search bar, type “Udemy” and press Enter.
3. **Install the App:** Tap on the Udemy app icon, then tap the “Install” button.
4. **Open the App:** Once the installation is complete, tap the “Open” button to launch the app.
5. **Sign Up or Log In:** Sign up or log in with your credentials to explore trending courses in your desired industry.

PharmEasy is an e-commerce platform that allows you to purchase medicines and healthcare products from the comfort of your home. Launched in 2014, the Mumbai-based PharmEasy currently serves over 25 lac registered customers and has successfully provided services to 18,000 plus PINs in India. Through the pharmacy store, you can be able to order your medicines at a lower price, ranging from 30% or more.

Apart from the medicinal products, PharmEasy also deals in diagnostic tests available from the leading laboratories in the country. You can collect test samples at your doorstep, with results available online. ## How Does PharmEasy Work?

Placing orders on PharmEasy is incredibly simple. To get the license, you only require a doctor’s prescription bearing the correct date, signature, etc., as may be required.
